import numpy as np
import joblib
import os
from sklearn.neural_network import MLPClassifier
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from sklearn.base import BaseEstimator, TransformerMixin

class DensityCenteringTransformer(BaseEstimator, TransformerMixin):
    """
    Centers data based on the highest density region (mode) of the distribution.
    This allows the classifier to be invariant to location shifts of the main population.
    """
    def __init__(self, bin_size=50):
        self.bin_size = bin_size
        self.reference_mode = None
        self.dataset_modes = {}

    def set_reference_mode(self, mode):
        """Sets a fixed mode to use for transformation (e.g. from the full dataset during feedback loop)."""
        self.reference_mode = np.array(mode)

    def _find_mode(self, X):
        if X.shape[0] < 5:
            if self.reference_mode is not None:
                return self.reference_mode
            return np.mean(X, axis=0)

        H, xedges, yedges = np.histogram2d(X[:, 0], X[:, 1], bins=self.bin_size)
        idx = np.unravel_index(np.argmax(H), H.shape)
        x_c = (xedges[idx[0]] + xedges[idx[0]+1]) / 2.0
        y_c = (yedges[idx[1]] + yedges[idx[1]+1]) / 2.0
        # Only return the center of the mode
        return np.array([x_c, y_c])

    def fit(self, X, y=None):
        return self

    def transform(self, X, dataset_ids=None):
        X = np.asarray(X)
        if dataset_ids is None:
            if self.reference_mode is not None:
                return X - self.reference_mode
            mode = self._find_mode(X)
            return X - mode

        X_out = np.zeros_like(X)
        dataset_ids = np.asarray(dataset_ids)
        unique_ids = np.unique(dataset_ids)
        for uid in unique_ids:
            mask = (dataset_ids == uid)
            X_sub = X[mask]

            key = str(uid)

            if X_sub.shape[0] >= 50:
                mode = self._find_mode(X_sub)
                self.dataset_modes[key] = mode
            elif key in self.dataset_modes:
                mode = self.dataset_modes[key]
            else:
                if self.reference_mode is not None:
                    mode = self.reference_mode
                else:
                    mode = self._find_mode(X_sub)

            X_out[mask] = X_sub - mode

        return X_out


class GatingClassifier:
    """
    A neural network classifier for distinguishing In-Gate (Live) vs Out-Gate (Debris/Outlier) events.
    Architecture:
    - Input: 2 Features (Log_FSC, Log_SSC) + 8 Cell Type Features (One-Hot)
    - Preprocessing: DensityCentering -> StandardScaler (on continuous) + OneHot (on categorical)
    - Network: MLP with 2 hidden layers (64, 32 neurons)
    - Activation: ReLU
    - Output: Binary classification probability
    """

    def __init__(self, hidden_layer_sizes=(64, 32), random_state=42):
        self.centerer = DensityCenteringTransformer()
        self.scaler = StandardScaler()
        self.mlp = MLPClassifier(
            hidden_layer_sizes=hidden_layer_sizes,
            activation='relu',
            solver='adam',
            alpha=0.0001,      # L2 regularization
            batch_size='auto', # Minibatch optimization
            learning_rate='adaptive',
            learning_rate_init=0.001,
            max_iter=500,
            early_stopping=True, # Important for active learning to prevent overfitting
            validation_fraction=0.1,
            random_state=random_state,
            verbose=False
        )
        self.is_fitted = False
        self.max_cell_types = 8
        self.cell_type_map = {'colo': 0}

    def _encode_cell_types(self, cell_types, n_samples):
        # Result placeholder
        X_cat = np.zeros((n_samples, self.max_cell_types))

        target_indices = []
        if cell_types is None:
            # Default to 'colo', which is index 0
            default_idx = self.cell_type_map.get('colo', 0)
            target_indices = [default_idx] * n_samples
        else:
            if isinstance(cell_types, str):
                cell_types = [cell_types] * n_samples

            for ct in cell_types:
                if ct not in self.cell_type_map:
                    if len(self.cell_type_map) < self.max_cell_types:
                        self.cell_type_map[ct] = len(self.cell_type_map)
                    else:
                        # Fallback to colo if we exceed capacity
                        pass
                target_indices.append(self.cell_type_map.get(ct, 0))

        X_cat[np.arange(n_samples), target_indices] = 1
        return X_cat

    def _prepare_input(self, X_centered_scaled, cell_types):
        # Handle backward compatibility for models trained on just 2 features
        if hasattr(self.mlp, 'n_features_in_') and self.mlp.n_features_in_ == 2:
            return X_centered_scaled

        dataset_len = X_centered_scaled.shape[0]
        X_cat = self._encode_cell_types(cell_types, dataset_len)
        return np.hstack([X_centered_scaled, X_cat])

    def set_context_mode(self, mode):
        """Pass context (dataset mode) to the centerer."""
        self.centerer.set_reference_mode(mode)

    def fit(self, X, y, dataset_ids=None, cell_types=None):
        """
        Train the model.
        Args:
            X: Features (Log_FSC, Log_SSC)
            y: Labels
            dataset_ids: Optional, array of dataset identifiers for correct per-dataset centering.
            cell_types: Optional, list of cell type strings.
        """
        # 1. Center
        X_centered = self.centerer.transform(X, dataset_ids=dataset_ids)

        # 2. Scale
        X_scaled = self.scaler.fit_transform(X_centered)

        # 3. Prepare
        X_final = self._prepare_input(X_scaled, cell_types)

        # 4. Train
        self.mlp.fit(X_final, y)
        self.is_fitted = True
        return self

    def partial_fit(self, X, y, classes=None, dataset_ids=None, cell_types=None):
        """
        Incremental update of the model.
        """
        if classes is None:
            classes = np.array([0, 1])

        # 1. Center
        X_centered = self.centerer.transform(X, dataset_ids=dataset_ids)

        # 2. Scale (Partial fit scaler)
        self.scaler.partial_fit(X_centered)
        X_scaled = self.scaler.transform(X_centered)

        # 3. Prepare
        X_final = self._prepare_input(X_scaled, cell_types)

        # 4. Update MLP
        self.mlp.partial_fit(X_final, y, classes=classes)
        self.is_fitted = True
        return self

    def predict(self, X, dataset_ids=None, cell_types=None):
        """Returns class predictions (0 or 1)."""
        if not self.is_fitted:
            # Fallback for unfitted predictions (e.g. init)
            pass

        X_centered = self.centerer.transform(X, dataset_ids=dataset_ids)
        X_scaled = self.scaler.transform(X_centered)
        X_final = self._prepare_input(X_scaled, cell_types)
        return self.mlp.predict(X_final)

    def predict_proba(self, X, dataset_ids=None, cell_types=None):
        """
        Returns probability of class 1 (Live).
        """
        X_centered = self.centerer.transform(X, dataset_ids=dataset_ids)
        X_scaled = self.scaler.transform(X_centered)
        X_final = self._prepare_input(X_scaled, cell_types)
        probs = self.mlp.predict_proba(X_final)
        return probs[:, 1]

    def score(self, X, y, dataset_ids=None, cell_types=None):
        """Returns accuracy."""
        X_centered = self.centerer.transform(X, dataset_ids=dataset_ids)
        X_scaled = self.scaler.transform(X_centered)
        X_final = self._prepare_input(X_scaled, cell_types)
        return self.mlp.score(X_final, y)

    def uncertainty(self, X, dataset_ids=None, cell_types=None):
        """
        Calculates uncertainty metric.
        """
        probs = self.predict_proba(X, dataset_ids=dataset_ids, cell_types=cell_types) # P(Live)
        uncertainty = 1.0 - np.abs(probs - 0.5) * 2.0
        return uncertainty

    def save(self, filepath):
        """Save model to disk."""
        directory = os.path.dirname(filepath)
        if directory and not os.path.exists(directory):
            os.makedirs(directory)

        # Save the whole object to preserve scaler state and configuration
        joblib.dump(self, filepath)

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, filepath):
        """Load model from disk."""
        if not os.path.exists(filepath):
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Model file not found: {filepath}")

        loaded = joblib.load(filepath)

        if isinstance(loaded, cls):
            # Migration check: ensure new attributes exist if loading old version
            if not hasattr(loaded, 'max_cell_types'):
                loaded.max_cell_types = 16
            if not hasattr(loaded, 'cell_type_map'):
                loaded.cell_type_map = {'colo': 0}
            return loaded
        elif hasattr(loaded, 'predict'):
            # Legacy Pipeline support
            instance = cls()
            instance.mlp = loaded # Assume loaded is the pipeline (or wrap it)
            # Disable our manual scaling/centering
            # Use compatible signature for DensityCenteringTransformer.transform(X, dataset_ids=None)
            instance.centerer.transform = lambda X, dataset_ids=None: X
            instance.scaler.transform = lambda X, copy=None: X
            instance.scaler.fit = lambda X, y=None, sample_weight=None: instance.scaler
            # Mark as fitted
            instance.is_fitted = True
            return instance

        return loaded


class SingletClassifier:
    """
    A neural network classifier for distinguishing Singlets vs Doublets.
    Architecture:
    - Input: 2 Features (Log_FSC-A, Log_FSC-H) + 8 Cell Type Features (One-Hot)
    - Preprocessing: DensityCentering -> StandardScaler (on continuous) + OneHot (on categorical)
    - Network: MLP with 2 hidden layers (64, 32 neurons)
    - Activation: ReLU
    - Output: Binary classification probability
    """

    def __init__(self, hidden_layer_sizes=(64, 32), random_state=42):
        self.centerer = DensityCenteringTransformer()
        self.scaler = StandardScaler()
        self.mlp = MLPClassifier(
            hidden_layer_sizes=hidden_layer_sizes,
            activation='relu',
            solver='adam',
            alpha=0.0001,      # L2 regularization
            batch_size='auto', # Minibatch optimization
            learning_rate='adaptive',
            learning_rate_init=0.001,
            max_iter=500,
            early_stopping=True,
            validation_fraction=0.1,
            random_state=random_state,
            verbose=False
        )
        self.is_fitted = False
        self.max_cell_types = 8
        self.cell_type_map = {'colo': 0}

    def _encode_cell_types(self, cell_types, n_samples):
        # Result placeholder
        X_cat = np.zeros((n_samples, self.max_cell_types))

        target_indices = []
        if cell_types is None:
            # Default to 'colo', which is index 0
            default_idx = self.cell_type_map.get('colo', 0)
            target_indices = [default_idx] * n_samples
        else:
            if isinstance(cell_types, str):
                cell_types = [cell_types] * n_samples

            for ct in cell_types:
                if ct not in self.cell_type_map:
                    if len(self.cell_type_map) < self.max_cell_types:
                        self.cell_type_map[ct] = len(self.cell_type_map)
                    else:
                        # Fallback to colo if we exceed capacity
                        pass
                target_indices.append(self.cell_type_map.get(ct, 0))

        X_cat[np.arange(n_samples), target_indices] = 1
        return X_cat

    def _prepare_input(self, X_centered_scaled, cell_types):
        # Handle backward compatibility for models trained on just 2 features
        if hasattr(self.mlp, 'n_features_in_') and self.mlp.n_features_in_ == 2:
            return X_centered_scaled

        dataset_len = X_centered_scaled.shape[0]
        X_cat = self._encode_cell_types(cell_types, dataset_len)
        return np.hstack([X_centered_scaled, X_cat])

    def set_context_mode(self, mode):
        """Pass context (dataset mode) to the centerer."""
        self.centerer.set_reference_mode(mode)

    def fit(self, X, y, dataset_ids=None, cell_types=None):
        """
        Train the model.
        Args:
            X: Features (usually Log_FSC-A, Log_FSC-H)
            y: Labels (1=Singlet, 0=Doublet)
            dataset_ids: Optional, array of dataset identifiers.
            cell_types: Optional, list of cell type strings.
        """
        # 1. Center
        X_centered = self.centerer.transform(X, dataset_ids=dataset_ids)

        # 2. Scale
        X_scaled = self.scaler.fit_transform(X_centered)

        # 3. Prepare
        X_final = self._prepare_input(X_scaled, cell_types)

        # 4. Train
        self.mlp.fit(X_final, y)
        self.is_fitted = True
        return self

    def partial_fit(self, X, y, classes=None, dataset_ids=None, cell_types=None):
        """
        Incremental update of the model.
        """
        if classes is None:
            classes = np.array([0, 1])

        # 1. Center
        X_centered = self.centerer.transform(X, dataset_ids=dataset_ids)

        # 2. Scale (Partial fit scaler)
        self.scaler.partial_fit(X_centered)
        X_scaled = self.scaler.transform(X_centered)

        # 3. Prepare
        X_final = self._prepare_input(X_scaled, cell_types)

        # 4. Update MLP
        self.mlp.partial_fit(X_final, y, classes=classes)
        self.is_fitted = True
        return self

    def predict(self, X, dataset_ids=None, cell_types=None):
        """Returns class predictions (0 or 1)."""
        if not self.is_fitted:
            pass

        X_centered = self.centerer.transform(X, dataset_ids=dataset_ids)
        X_scaled = self.scaler.transform(X_centered)
        X_final = self._prepare_input(X_scaled, cell_types)
        return self.mlp.predict(X_final)

    def predict_proba(self, X, dataset_ids=None, cell_types=None):
        """
        Returns probability of class 1 (Singlet).
        """
        X_centered = self.centerer.transform(X, dataset_ids=dataset_ids)
        X_scaled = self.scaler.transform(X_centered)
        X_final = self._prepare_input(X_scaled, cell_types)
        probs = self.mlp.predict_proba(X_final)
        return probs[:, 1]

    def score(self, X, y, dataset_ids=None, cell_types=None):
        """Returns accuracy."""
        X_centered = self.centerer.transform(X, dataset_ids=dataset_ids)
        X_scaled = self.scaler.transform(X_centered)
        X_final = self._prepare_input(X_scaled, cell_types)
        return self.mlp.score(X_final, y)

    def uncertainty(self, X, dataset_ids=None, cell_types=None):
        """
        Calculates uncertainty metric.
        """
        probs = self.predict_proba(X, dataset_ids=dataset_ids, cell_types=cell_types) # P(Singlet)
        uncertainty = 1.0 - np.abs(probs - 0.5) * 2.0
        return uncertainty

    def save(self, filepath):
        """Save model to disk."""
        directory = os.path.dirname(filepath)
        if directory and not os.path.exists(directory):
            os.makedirs(directory)
        joblib.dump(self, filepath)

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, filepath):
        """Load model from disk."""
        if not os.path.exists(filepath):
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Model file not found: {filepath}")

        loaded = joblib.load(filepath)

        if isinstance(loaded, cls):
            # Migration check
            if not hasattr(loaded, 'max_cell_types'):
                loaded.max_cell_types = 16
            if not hasattr(loaded, 'cell_type_map'):
                loaded.cell_type_map = {'colo': 0}
            return loaded

        return loaded

import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import os
import sys
import argparse

# Allow import from relative path when running as script
if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.path.append(os.getcwd())
    from glow.active_learning.models import GatingClassifier
else:
    from .models import GatingClassifier

def plot_classification_result(dataset_id, data_path="data/synthetic_training_data.parquet", model_path="data/gating_model_full.pkl", output_file=None):
    """
    Plots the MLP classification classification vs Ground Truth for a specific dataset.
    Args:
        dataset_id (int): The ID of the dataset to visualize (0-999).
        data_path (str): Path to the parquet data file.
        model_path (str): Path to the saved model pickle.
        output_file (str): Path to save the plot. If None, shows the plot.
    """
    print(f"Loading data for Dataset {dataset_id} from {data_path}...")
    if not os.path.exists(data_path):
        # try relative path if running from subdir
        if os.path.exists(os.path.join("..", "..", data_path)):
             data_path = os.path.join("..", "..", data_path)
        else:
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Data file not found: {data_path}")

    try:
        # Check if we can use filters (pyarrow required, usually present)
        # Check for DatasetID, if likely to exist
        try:
             df = pd.read_parquet(data_path, filters=[('DatasetID', '=', dataset_id)])
        except Exception:
             # If filters fail (e.g. column missing in schema causing error before fallback), just load all
             df = pd.read_parquet(data_path)
             if 'DatasetID' in df.columns:
                 df = df[df['DatasetID'] == dataset_id]
             else:
                 print("Warning: 'DatasetID' column not found, using all data.")

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error loading data: {e}")
        return

    if df.empty:
        print(f"No data found for DatasetID {dataset_id}")
        return

    print(f"Loaded {len(df)} events.")

    # Load Model (Ensure class is importable)
    if not os.path.exists(model_path):
         if os.path.exists(os.path.join("..", "..", model_path)):
             model_path = os.path.join("..", "..", model_path)

    print(f"Loading model from {model_path}...")
    try:
        model = GatingClassifier.load(model_path)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error loading model: {e}")
        return

    # Predict
    # Ensure columns exist
    if "Log_FSC" not in df.columns:
        # Note: model expects log1p now as per train_model.py
        if "FSC-A" in df.columns:
            df["Log_FSC"] = np.log1p(df["FSC-A"])
        else:
            print("Error: FSC-A column missing")
            return

        if "SSC-A" in df.columns:
            df["Log_SSC"] = np.log1p(df["SSC-A"])
        else:
            print("Error: SSC-A column missing")
            return

    X = df[["Log_FSC", "Log_SSC"]].values

    # Predict
    y_pred = model.predict(X)

    # Ground Truth
    has_ground_truth = False
    acc_text = "N/A"

    if "TrueLabel" in df.columns:
        y_true_raw = df["TrueLabel"].values
        # Check if all are Unknown
        if np.all(y_true_raw == 'Unknown'):
            acc_text = "N/A (Unknown Labels)"
            has_ground_truth = True # We have the column, but it's all unknown
        else:
            # Convert 'Live' to 1, others to 0
            y_true = np.zeros(len(y_true_raw), dtype=int)
            y_true[y_true_raw == 'Live'] = 1
            has_ground_truth = True

            acc = np.mean(y_pred == y_true)
            acc_text = f"{acc:.2%}"

    print(f"Dataset {dataset_id} Accuracy: {acc_text}")

    # Plotting
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(16, 7))

    # Common settings
    fs = 12
    alpha = 0.6
    s = 5

    # 1. MLP Prediction
    ax1 = axes[0]
    colors_pred = np.where(y_pred == 1, 'blue', 'red')

    ax1.scatter(df["FSC-A"], df["SSC-A"], c=colors_pred, s=s, alpha=alpha, label='Prediction')
    ax1.set_xscale("log")
    ax1.set_yscale("log")
    ax1.set_title(f"MLP Prediction (Dataset #{dataset_id})\nAccuracy: {acc_text}", fontsize=fs)
    ax1.set_xlabel("FSC-A (Linear)", fontsize=fs)
    ax1.set_ylabel("SSC-A (Linear)", fontsize=fs)
    ax1.grid(True, alpha=0.3)

    from matplotlib.lines import Line2D
    legend_1 = [
        Line2D([0],[0], marker="o", color="w", markerfacecolor="blue", label="Pred: In-Gate (Live)"),
        Line2D([0],[0], marker="o", color="w", markerfacecolor="red", label="Pred: Out-Gate")
    ]
    ax1.legend(handles=legend_1, loc='upper left')

    # 2. Ground Truth
    ax2 = axes[1]
    if has_ground_truth:
        label_colors = {'Live': 'green', 'Debris': 'black', 'Outliers': 'orange', 'Unknown': 'gray'}
        unique_labels = df['TrueLabel'].unique()

        for lbl in unique_labels:
            mask = df['TrueLabel'] == lbl
            # Convert category to string if needed
            lbl_str = str(lbl)
            c = label_colors.get(lbl_str, 'gray')
            ax2.scatter(df.loc[mask, "FSC-A"], df.loc[mask, "SSC-A"], c=c, s=s, alpha=alpha, label=lbl_str)
    else:
        ax2.text(0.5, 0.5, "No Ground Truth Available", ha='center', va='center', transform=ax2.transAxes)

    ax2.set_xscale("log")
    ax2.set_yscale("log")
    ax2.set_title(f"Ground Truth (Dataset #{dataset_id})", fontsize=fs)
    ax2.set_xlabel("FSC-A (Linear)", fontsize=fs)
    ax2.set_ylabel("SSC-A (Linear)", fontsize=fs)
    ax2.grid(True, alpha=0.3)
    ax2.legend(loc='upper left')

    plt.tight_layout()

    if output_file:
        plt.savefig(output_file)
        print(f"Plot saved to {output_file}")
    else:
        plt.show()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Visualize MLP Classification vs Ground Truth")
    parser.add_argument("dataset_id", type=int, nargs="?", default=42, help="ID of the dataset to visualize (default: 42)")
    parser.add_argument("--data", type=str, default="data/synthetic_training_data.parquet", help="Path to data parquet file")
    parser.add_argument("--model", type=str, default="data/gating_model_full.pkl", help="Path to model pickle file")
    parser.add_argument("--output", "-o", type=str, default="mlp_vis_check.png", help="Output filename (default: mlp_vis_check.png)")

    args = parser.parse_args()

    plot_classification_result(args.dataset_id, data_path=args.data, model_path=args.model, output_file=args.output)

FILE: glow/active_learning/simulation.py

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import cytoflow
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import os
import sys
from sklearn.cluster import DBSCAN
from sklearn.mixture import GaussianMixture
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler

# Ensure we can import glow
sys.path.append(os.getcwd())

from glow.gating.strategies import DensityGating, OutlierDetector

def analyze_data_for_gating(data_df, channels):
    """
    Analyzes the data structure to determine optimal outlier removal and keep ratio.
    Removes hard-coded intensity thresholds in favor of relative clustering logic.
    """
    print("  -> Analyzing data structure (Automatic Mode)...")
    X = data_df[channels].values

    # Standardize for clustering stability
    scaler = StandardScaler()
    X_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(X)

    # Compute a simple 1D "Brightness Score" for ranking (Sum of standardized Log channels)
    # This aligns with the diagonal elongation of populations.
    brightness_scores = X_scaled.sum(axis=1)

    # --- Step 1: DBSCAN for main topological features ---
    db = DBSCAN(eps=0.3, min_samples=50).fit(X_scaled)
    labels = db.labels_

    unique_labels = set(labels)
    if -1 in unique_labels:
        unique_labels.remove(-1)

    clusters = []
    noise_mask = (labels == -1)

    # Collect Cluster Stats
    for label in unique_labels:
        mask = (labels == label)
        count = np.sum(mask)
        mean_brightness = np.mean(brightness_scores[mask])

        clusters.append({
            "id": label,
            "count": count,
            "brightness": mean_brightness,
            "mask": mask
        })

    print(f"    DBSCAN found {len(clusters)} clusters.")
    for c in clusters:
        print(f"      Cluster {c['id']}: N={c['count']}, Brightness={c['brightness']:.2f}")

    # Initialize outlier_low estimate
    suggested_outlier_low = 0.0

    # --- Step 2: Identify Debris & Cutoff ---
    if len(clusters) == 0:
        print("    WARNING: No clusters found. Assuming sparse data/noise.")
        suggested_outlier_low = np.mean(noise_mask)

    elif len(clusters) == 1:
        print("    Single cluster detected (Live+Debris merged). Running GMM for internal separation...")
        # Use GMM on the whole dataset (or just the cluster) to separate modes
        # We use the global X_scaled for consistency
        c = clusters[0]
        mask = c['mask']
        X_cluster = X_scaled[mask]

        gmm = GaussianMixture(n_components=2, random_state=42)
        gmm.fit(X_cluster)

        # Analyze components
        means = np.asarray(gmm.means_) # Shape (2, 2)
        comp_brightness = np.sum(means, axis=1) # Projected means

        idx_sorted = np.argsort(comp_brightness)
        low_idx = int(idx_sorted[0])
        high_idx = int(idx_sorted[1])

        mu_debris = float(comp_brightness[low_idx])
        mu_live = float(comp_brightness[high_idx])

        weights = np.asarray(gmm.weights_)
        weight_debris = float(weights[low_idx])

        print(f"      Debris Mode: {mu_debris:.2f} (Weight={weight_debris:.1%})")
        print(f"      Live Mode:   {mu_live:.2f}")

        # --- RECALL OPTIMIZATION ---
        # User wants to capture as many Live cells as possible, even overlapping ones.
        # We place the cut boundary "conservatively" - closer to the Debris center than the Live center.
        # Standard midpoint would be 0.5. We use 0.35 to bias towards keeping data.
        # boundary = mu_debris + 0.35 * (mu_live - mu_debris)

        # Ideally, we want to cut the "core" of the debris, effectively using the GMM to tell us
        # where the debris density drops off.
        # A simple heuristic: Cut where the Debris is dominant?
        # Let's try to set the cut at 30% of the distance from Debris to Live.
        boundary_val = mu_debris + 0.30 * (mu_live - mu_debris)

        # Calculate what percentile of the data falls below this boundary
        # This translates our "Intensity Cut" into a "Quantile Cut" for OutlierDetector
        n_below_boundary = np.sum(brightness_scores < boundary_val)
        suggested_outlier_low = n_below_boundary / len(data_df)

        print(f"      Calculated 'Safe' Cutoff Boundary: {boundary_val:.2f} (Percentile: {suggested_outlier_low:.1%})")

    else:
        # Multiple clusters. We assume the darkest one is Debris.
        sorted_clusters = sorted(clusters, key=lambda c: c['brightness'])
        debris_cluster = sorted_clusters[0]

        # In a multi-cluster scenario, the debris cluster is likely well-separated.
        # However, to be safe (maximize recall), we should check if we really want to cut *all* of it?
        # Usually yes, if it's a distinct cluster.
        # But let's count the noise + debris_cluster count.
        total_debris = debris_cluster["count"] + np.sum(noise_mask)
        suggested_outlier_low = total_debris / len(data_df)
        print(f"    lowest-intensity cluster ({debris_cluster['id']}) identified as Debris.")

    # --- Step 3: Configure Parameters ---
    suggested_outlier_low = min(0.60, suggested_outlier_low)
    suggested_outlier_low = max(0.01, suggested_outlier_low)

    # Keep Ratio:
    # Since we are being conservative with outlier removal (allowing some debris in),
    # we should trust DensityGating to find the peak.
    # 0.95 keeps the core 95%.
    ratio = 0.95

    print(f"  -> Final Suggested outlier_low: {suggested_outlier_low:.3f}")
    print(f"  -> Final Suggested keep ratio: {ratio:.3f}")

    return suggested_outlier_low, ratio


def generate_synthetic_data(n_samples=5000):
    """
    Generates synthetic flow cytometry data.
    """
    # 1. Randomize Proportions
    prop_outliers = np.random.uniform(0.01, 0.05)
    remaining = 1.0 - prop_outliers

    prop_main = np.random.uniform(0.50, remaining - 0.1)
    prop_debris = remaining - prop_main

    n_main = int(n_samples * prop_main)
    n_debris = int(n_samples * prop_debris)
    n_outliers = n_samples - n_main - n_debris

    print(f"Generating {n_samples} events:")
    print(f"  Live: {n_main} ({prop_main:.1%})")
    print(f"  Debris: {n_debris} ({prop_debris:.1%})")

    # 2. Debris: Concentrated near Log10 5.5 - 6.5 (Real data range matches ~10^6 range)
    debris_fsc_center = np.random.uniform(5.5, 6.2)
    debris_ssc_center = np.random.uniform(5.5, 6.2)

    # Dense (sigma 0.15-0.25 in Log10 space -> ~0.35-0.6 in Ln space)
    sigma_debris = np.random.uniform(0.15, 0.25)
    cov_debris = [[sigma_debris**2, 0.5*sigma_debris*sigma_debris],
                  [0.5*sigma_debris*sigma_debris, sigma_debris**2]]

    debris_data = np.random.multivariate_normal(
        [debris_fsc_center, debris_ssc_center], cov_debris, n_debris
    )

    # 3. Live: Connected, More Elongated -- Center around 7.5 - 7.9 (Real data mean ~7.7)
    # dist_offset ensures separation but we want explicit placement
    live_fsc_center = np.random.uniform(7.5, 7.9)
    live_ssc_center = np.random.uniform(7.3, 7.7) # SSC usually slightly lower

    # Elongated - tighter distribution
    sigma_live_fsc = np.random.uniform(0.15, 0.25)
    sigma_live_ssc = np.random.uniform(0.15, 0.25)
    correlation = np.random.uniform(0.6, 0.8)

    cov_main = [
        [sigma_live_fsc**2, correlation * sigma_live_fsc * sigma_live_ssc],
        [correlation * sigma_live_fsc * sigma_live_ssc, sigma_live_ssc**2]
    ]

    main_data = np.random.multivariate_normal(
        [live_fsc_center, live_ssc_center], cov_main, n_main
    )

    # 4. Outliers - Spread across the high range
    out_fsc = np.random.uniform(8.0, 9.0)
    out_ssc = np.random.uniform(8.0, 9.0)
    cov_out = [[0.2, 0], [0, 0.2]]
    out_cluster_data = np.random.multivariate_normal([out_fsc, out_ssc], cov_out, int(n_outliers/2))

    out_noise_data = np.random.uniform(4.0, 9.5, (n_outliers - int(n_outliers/2), 2))

    # Combine
    all_data_log = np.vstack([main_data, debris_data, out_cluster_data, out_noise_data])
    all_data_log = np.maximum(all_data_log, -1.0)

    all_data_linear = 10**all_data_log

    df = pd.DataFrame(all_data_linear, columns=["FSC-A", "SSC-A"])

    labels = (["Live"] * n_main) + \
             (["Debris"] * n_debris) + \
             (["Outliers"] * len(out_cluster_data)) + \
             (["Outliers"] * len(out_noise_data))
    df["TrueLabel"] = labels

    return df

def run_simulation():
    df = generate_synthetic_data(5000)

    # Log Channels
    df["Log_FSC"] = np.log10(df["FSC-A"] + 1e-1)
    df["Log_SSC"] = np.log10(df["SSC-A"] + 1e-1)

    df["Sample"] = "Synthetic"

    outlier_low, keep_ratio = analyze_data_for_gating(df, ["Log_FSC", "Log_SSC"])

    # Gating
    od = OutlierDetector(method="quantile", outlier_low=outlier_low, outlier_high=0.99)
    gating = DensityGating(name="Live", channels=["Log_FSC", "Log_SSC"], keep=keep_ratio, sigma=0.5, outlier_detector=od)

    exp = cytoflow.Experiment(data=df, metadata={"Sample": "category"})
    gated_exp = gating.apply(exp)

    # Eval
    is_live = gated_exp.data["Live"]

    true_live = (df["TrueLabel"] == "Live")
    recall = (is_live & true_live).sum() / true_live.sum()
    precision = (is_live & true_live).sum() / is_live.sum()

    print("Evaluation:")
    print(f"  Yield (Predicted Live): {is_live.sum()} / {len(df)} ({is_live.mean():.1%})")
    print(f"  Recall (True Live Recovered): {recall:.1%}")
    print(f"  Precision (True Live / Predicted Live): {precision:.1%}")

    # Plot
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(16, 8))

    # Plot 1: Gating
    ax1 = axes[0]
    ax1.scatter(df["FSC-A"], df["SSC-A"], c=np.where(is_live, "blue", "red"), s=5, alpha=0.5)
    ax1.set_xscale("log")
    ax1.set_yscale("log")
    ax1.set_xlabel("FSC-A")
    ax1.set_ylabel("SSC-A")
    ax1.set_title(f"Gating Prediction\nRecall={recall:.2f}, Precision={precision:.2f}")

    # Plot 2: Ground Truth
    ax2 = axes[1]
    colors = {"Live": "green", "Debris": "black", "Outliers": "orange"}
    for lbl, col in colors.items():
        subset = df[df["TrueLabel"] == lbl]
        ax2.scatter(subset["FSC-A"], subset["SSC-A"], c=col, s=5, alpha=0.5, label=lbl)
    ax2.set_xscale("log")
    ax2.set_yscale("log")
    ax2.set_xlabel("FSC-A")
    ax2.set_ylabel("SSC-A")
    ax2.legend()
    ax2.set_title("Ground Truth")

    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig("density_gating_simulation.png")
    print("Saved density_gating_simulation.png")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_simulation()


==================================================
FILE: glow/active_learning/learner.py
==================================================
import os
import shutil
import numpy as np
from .storage import ActiveLearningLogger
from .models import GatingClassifier, SingletClassifier

class ActiveLearner:
    """
    Manages the Active Learning process:
    1. Loads the Neural Network model.
    2. Queries for uncertain samples.
    3. Updates the model with new user feedback.
    """
    def __init__(self, storage_dir="data/active_learning_data", model_path="data/gating_model_full.pkl", model_type="gating"):
        self.storage_dir = storage_dir
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.model_type = model_type

        # Select Class
        if model_type == 'singlet':
            self.model_class = SingletClassifier
        else:
            self.model_class = GatingClassifier

        # Ensure backup exists for reset functionality
        self.backup_path = self.model_path + ".base"
        if os.path.exists(self.model_path) and not os.path.exists(self.backup_path):
            print(f"Creating base backup at {self.backup_path}")
            shutil.copy2(self.model_path, self.backup_path)

        self.logger = ActiveLearningLogger(storage_dir)
        self.model = self._load_or_init_model()

    def _load_or_init_model(self):
        """Loads model from disk or creates valid fallback if missing."""
        if os.path.exists(self.model_path):
            try:
                print(f"Loading Active Learning model ({self.model_type}) from {self.model_path}")
                return self.model_class.load(self.model_path)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error loading model: {e}")

        print(f"Initializing new/empty {self.model_class.__name__}.")
        return self.model_class()

    def reset_model(self):
        """
        Resets the model to the base state (from .base backup).
        Returns:
            bool: Success status.
        """
        if not os.path.exists(self.backup_path):
            print("Cannot reset: Base backup file not found.")
            return False

        print(f"Resetting model from {self.backup_path}...")
        try:
            # Restore file
            shutil.copy2(self.backup_path, self.model_path)
            # Reload object
            self.model = self.model_class.load(self.model_path)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error resetting model: {e}")
            return False

    def predict(self, df):
        """
        Predicts labels for a dataframe.
        """
        X = self._extract_features(df)
        return self.model.predict(X)

    def query_uncertainty(self, df, n_instances=10):
        """
        Selects the top N most uncertain instances for labeling.
        """
        X = self._extract_features(df)
        uncertainties = self.model.uncertainty(X)
        query_indices = np.argsort(uncertainties)[::-1][:n_instances]
        return df.iloc[query_indices], query_indices

    def teach(self, X_new, y_new):
        """
        Updates the model with new labeled data.
        """
        if len(X_new) == 0:
            return

        print(f"Teaching model with {len(X_new)} new samples...")

        # 1. Update Model (Incremental)
        try:
            self.model.partial_fit(X_new, y_new)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Partial fit failed ({e}).")
            return

        # 2. Save Model checkpoint
        self.model.save(self.model_path)

        # 3. Log/Save this training batch
        self.logger.save_labeled_batch(X_new, y_new, source=f"active_learning_feedback_{self.model_type}")

    def get_learned_gate(self, cell_line, gate_type):
        """
        Retrieves the most recent manual gate vertices for a specific cell line and gate type
        from the feedback logs.
        """
        feedback = self.logger.load_feedback()
        # Filter for cell_line and gate_type
        relevant = [
            f for f in feedback
            if f.get('cell_line') == cell_line and f.get('gate_type') == gate_type and f.get('gate_params')
        ]

        if not relevant:
            return None

        # Return the most recent one
        latest = relevant[-1]
        return latest['gate_params']

    def _extract_features(self, df):
        """Helper to safely get Features based on model type"""
        if self.model_type == 'singlet':
            # Expecting Log_FSC_A, Log_FSC_H usually, or just 2 cols
            # If explicit columns exist, use them
            if "Log_FSC_A" in df.columns and "Log_FSC_H" in df.columns:
                 return df[["Log_FSC_A", "Log_FSC_H"]].values

            # Fallback: Just take the first 2 columns if not named specifically?
            # Or try to construct from standard names if passed
            pass

        # Default / Gating behavior
        if "Log_FSC" in df.columns:
             return df[["Log_FSC", "Log_SSC"]].values

        # Generic Fallback: Use values of dataframe directly if 2 columns
        if df.shape[1] == 2:
            return df.values

        raise ValueError(f"Dataframe missing required feature columns for {self.model_type}.")

FILE: glow/active_learning/active_learning_scripts/generate_training_data_parquet.py

import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
import os
import sys
from tqdm import tqdm

# Ensure we can import glow
sys.path.append(os.getcwd())

from glow.active_learning.simulation import generate_synthetic_data

def generate_large_dataset(n_datasets=1000, n_samples_per_dataset=5000, output_path="data/synthetic_training_data.parquet"):
    print(f"Generating {n_datasets} datasets with {n_samples_per_dataset} samples each...")

    all_dfs = []

    for i in tqdm(range(n_datasets)):
        # Generate single dataset
        df = generate_synthetic_data(n_samples=n_samples_per_dataset)

        # Add DatasetID column
        df["DatasetID"] = i

        # Optimize types to save memory
        df["DatasetID"] = df["DatasetID"].astype(np.int32)
        df["TrueLabel"] = df["TrueLabel"].astype("category")

        all_dfs.append(df)

    print("Concatenating...")
    full_df = pd.concat(all_dfs, ignore_index=True)

    # Ensure data directory exists
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(output_path), exist_ok=True)

    print(f"Saving to {output_path}...")
    full_df.to_parquet(output_path, index=False)

    print(f"Done! Shape: {full_df.shape}")
    print(f"Columns: {full_df.columns.tolist()}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    generate_large_dataset()


==================================================
FILE: glow/active_learning/active_learning_scripts/train_singlet_model.py
==================================================
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
import os
import sys
import argparse
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split
from sklearn.metrics import classification_report, confusion_matrix, accuracy_score

# Add project root to path
sys.path.append(os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '../../..')))

from glow.active_learning.models import SingletClassifier

def train_singlet_model(data_path, output_path, subsample=None):
    print(f"Loading data from {data_path}...")
    if not os.path.exists(data_path):
        print(f"Error: Data file not found at {data_path}")
        return

    df = pd.read_parquet(data_path)

    # Drop NAs
    df = df.dropna()

    print(f"Loaded {len(df)} samples.")

    if subsample and len(df) > subsample:
        print(f"Subsampling to {subsample} samples...")
        df = df.sample(n=subsample, random_state=42)

    # Features: Log_FSC_A, Log_FSC_H
    X = df[['Log_FSC_A', 'Log_FSC_H']].values
    y = df['Label'].values
    dataset_ids = df['DatasetID'].values
    cell_types = df['CellType'].values if 'CellType' in df.columns else None

    if cell_types is None:
        print("Warning: CellType column not found in data. Training without cell type info.")

    # Split Train/Val (80/20)
    print("Splitting data 80/20...")
    if cell_types is not None:
        X_train, X_val, y_train, y_val, id_train, id_val, ct_train, ct_val = train_test_split(
            X, y, dataset_ids, cell_types, test_size=0.2, random_state=42, stratify=y
        )
    else:
        X_train, X_val, y_train, y_val, id_train, id_val = train_test_split(
            X, y, dataset_ids, test_size=0.2, random_state=42, stratify=y
        )
        ct_train = None
        ct_val = None

    print(f"Training set: {len(X_train)} samples")
    print(f"Validation set: {len(X_val)} samples")

    # Initialize model
    print("Initializing model...")
    model = SingletClassifier(hidden_layer_sizes=(64, 32), random_state=42)

    # Configure MLP for monitoring and early stopping
    model.mlp.verbose = True
    model.mlp.early_stopping = True
    model.mlp.n_iter_no_change = 5

    print("Starting training...")
    # Passing dataset_ids is crucial for DensityCentering
    model.fit(X_train, y_train, dataset_ids=id_train, cell_types=ct_train)
    print("Training complete.")

    # Evaluation
    print("Evaluating on Validation Set...")
    y_pred = model.predict(X_val, dataset_ids=id_val, cell_types=ct_val)

    acc = accuracy_score(y_val, y_pred)
    print(f"Validation Accuracy: {acc:.4%}")

    print("\nClassification Report:")
    print(classification_report(y_val, y_pred, target_names=['Doublet', 'Singlet']))

    print("\nConfusion Matrix:")
    print(confusion_matrix(y_val, y_pred))

    # Save Model
    print(f"\nSaving model to {output_path}...")
    model.save(output_path)
    print("Model saved.")

    # Plotting Loss Curve
    if hasattr(model.mlp, 'loss_curve_'):
        print("Generating loss curve...")
        plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))
        plt.plot(model.mlp.loss_curve_, label='Training Loss')

        # If early stopping was used, validation scores might be available in validation_scores_
        if hasattr(model.mlp, 'validation_scores_') and model.mlp.validation_scores_:
             plt.plot(model.mlp.validation_scores_, label='Validation Score (Accuracy)')

        plt.title('Singlet Classifier Training Loss Curve')
        plt.xlabel('Iterations')
        plt.ylabel('Loss')
        plt.legend()
        plt.grid(True)

        # Determine plot path
        plot_dir = os.path.dirname(output_path)
        if not plot_dir:
            plot_dir = "."
        plot_path = os.path.join(plot_dir, "singlet_training_loss_curve.png")

        plt.savefig(plot_path)
        print(f"Saved loss curve to {plot_path}")
    else:
        print("Warning: Loss curve not found in model attributes.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Train Singlet Classifier")
    parser.add_argument("--data", type=str, default="data/singlet_training_data.parquet", help="Path to input parquet data")
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=str, default="data/singlet_model_full.pkl", help="Path to save model")
    parser.add_argument("--subsample", type=int, default=1000000, help="Number of samples to train on (for speed)")

    args = parser.parse_args()
    train_singlet_model(args.data, args.output, args.subsample)


==================================================
FILE: glow/active_learning/active_learning_scripts/generate_training_data.py
==================================================
import os
import sys
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np

# Ensure glow is in path
sys.path.append(os.getcwd())

from glow.active_learning.simulation import generate_synthetic_data

OUTPUT_DIR = "data"
OUTPUT_FILE = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, "synthetic_training_data.csv")
N_DATASETS = 1000
N_POINTS = 5000

if not os.path.exists(OUTPUT_DIR):
    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR)

all_dfs = []
print(f"Generating {N_DATASETS} datasets of {N_POINTS} points...")

for i in range(N_DATASETS):
    # Suppress print output from generate_synthetic_data using a context manager or just let it print if checking progress?
    # The function prints to stdout. 1000 datasets * 4 lines = 4000 lines.
    # That's a lot of spam. I should redirect stdout temporarily.

    # Simple stdout capture
    with open(os.devnull, 'w') as fnull:
        original_stdout = sys.stdout
        sys.stdout = fnull
        try:
            df = generate_synthetic_data(N_POINTS)
        finally:
            sys.stdout = original_stdout

    # Process labels
    # In-gate: Live, Out-gate: Debris, Outliers
    df['Label'] = (df['TrueLabel'] == 'Live').astype(int)
    df['DatasetID'] = i

    # Add Log columns for convenience
    df["Log_FSC"] = np.log10(df["FSC-A"] + 1e-1)
    df["Log_SSC"] = np.log10(df["SSC-A"] + 1e-1)

    # Select columns
    keep_cols = ["DatasetID", "FSC-A", "SSC-A", "Log_FSC", "Log_SSC", "Label", "TrueLabel"]
    all_dfs.append(df[keep_cols])

    if (i+1) % 100 == 0:
        print(f"  Generated {i+1}/{N_DATASETS} datasets")

final_df = pd.concat(all_dfs, ignore_index=True)
print(f"Saving {len(final_df)} rows to {OUTPUT_FILE}...")
final_df.to_csv(OUTPUT_FILE, index=False)
print("Done.")


==================================================
FILE: glow/active_learning/active_learning_scripts/generate_real_data_parquet.py
==================================================
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
import os
import glob
import sys
from tqdm import tqdm

def load_all_labeled_data(
    base_path="/home/xwz/Projects/test/Glow/training/labeled_data/",
    output_path="data/real_training_data.parquet"
):
    print(f"Scanning {base_path}...")

    # Define cell types to look for based on folder names
    # User mentioned: colo, hek, hug1n, snu
    # We can just look at all subdirectories that contain csv.gz files

    all_files = glob.glob(os.path.join(base_path, "*", "*.csv.gz"))
    if not all_files:
        print("No .csv.gz files found.")
        return

    print(f"Found {len(all_files)} files total.")

    dfs = []
    dataset_counter = 0

    # Mapping for consistent column names
    # Based on: filter found in 'zcat ...' output earlier
    fsc_col_candidates = ['FSC 488/10-A', 'FSC-A', 'FSC 488/10-A']
    ssc_col_candidates = ['SSC 488/10-A', 'SSC-A', 'SSC 488/10-A']
    label_col = 'cells'

    # We need to ensure we handle the cell type mapping correctly if the model expects specific strings.
    # The prompt implies using folder names as cell type names.

    for file_path in tqdm(all_files):
        try:
            # Extract metadata
            folder_name = os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(file_path))
            cell_type = folder_name.lower() # e.g. 'colo', 'hek'

            # Load CSV
            df = pd.read_csv(file_path)

            # Identify columns
            fsc_col = next((c for c in fsc_col_candidates if c in df.columns), None)
            ssc_col = next((c for c in ssc_col_candidates if c in df.columns), None)

            if not fsc_col or not ssc_col:
                # Fallback fuzzy search if exact match fails
                for c in df.columns:
                    if 'FSC' in c and '-A' in c: fsc_col = c
                    if 'SSC' in c and '-A' in c: ssc_col = c

            if not fsc_col or not ssc_col or label_col not in df.columns:
                print(f"Skipping {file_path}: Missing FSC/SSC/cells columns.")
                continue

            # Process Data
            # 1. Log transform features (with simple epsilon/cutoff for log)
            # Flow values can be negative or zero sometimes after compensation? usually raw is > 0.
            # We assume raw linear data here.

            # Filter valid positive data for Log
            valid_mask = (df[fsc_col] > 0) & (df[ssc_col] > 0)
            clean_df = df.loc[valid_mask].copy()

            if len(clean_df) == 0:
                continue

            clean_df['Log_FSC'] = np.log10(clean_df[fsc_col])
            clean_df['Log_SSC'] = np.log10(clean_df[ssc_col])

            # 2. Labels
            # 'cells' column is Boolean. True -> Live (1), False -> Debris (0)
            clean_df['Label'] = clean_df[label_col].astype(int)

            # 3. Metadata
            clean_df['DatasetID'] = dataset_counter
            clean_df['CellType'] = cell_type

            # Select only needed columns to save memory
            subset = clean_df[['Log_FSC', 'Log_SSC', 'Label', 'DatasetID', 'CellType']]

            # Optimize types
            subset['DatasetID'] = subset['DatasetID'].astype(np.int32)
            subset['Label'] = subset['Label'].astype(np.int8)
            subset['Log_FSC'] = subset['Log_FSC'].astype(np.float32)
            subset['Log_SSC'] = subset['Log_SSC'].astype(np.float32)

            dfs.append(subset)
            dataset_counter += 1

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error processing {file_path}: {e}")

    if not dfs:
        print("No valid data extracted.")
        return

    print("Concatenating all datasets...")
    full_df = pd.concat(dfs, ignore_index=True)

    print(f"Total samples: {len(full_df)}")
    print(f"breakdown by CellType:\n{full_df['CellType'].value_counts()}")

    # Save
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(output_path), exist_ok=True)
    full_df.to_parquet(output_path, index=False)
    print(f"Saved training data to {output_path}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    load_all_labeled_data()


==================================================
FILE: glow/active_learning/active_learning_scripts/generate_singlet_training_data.py
==================================================
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
import os
import glob
from tqdm import tqdm

def generate_singlet_data(
    base_path="/home/xwz/Projects/test/Glow/training/labeled_data/",
    output_path="data/singlet_training_data.parquet"
):
    print(f"Scanning {base_path} for .csv.gz files...")
    all_files = glob.glob(os.path.join(base_path, "*", "*.csv.gz"))

    if not all_files:
        print("No .csv.gz files found.")
        return

    print(f"Found {len(all_files)} files total.")

    dfs = []
    dataset_counter = 0

    # Column candidates
    fsc_a_candidates = ['FSC 488/10-A', 'FSC-A']
    fsc_h_candidates = ['FSC 488/10-H', 'FSC-H']
    label_col = 'singlets'

    for file_path in tqdm(all_files):
        try:
            # Metadata
            folder_name = os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(file_path))

            # Load
            df = pd.read_csv(file_path)

            # Find columns
            fsc_a = next((c for c in fsc_a_candidates if c in df.columns), None)
            fsc_h = next((c for c in fsc_h_candidates if c in df.columns), None)

            # Fuzzy match fallback
            if not fsc_a:
                fsc_a = next((c for c in df.columns if 'FSC' in c and '-A' in c), None)
            if not fsc_h:
                fsc_h = next((c for c in df.columns if 'FSC' in c and '-H' in c), None)

            if not fsc_a or not fsc_h:
                # print(f"Skipping {file_path}: Missing FSC-A/H columns.")
                continue

            if label_col not in df.columns:
                # print(f"Skipping {file_path}: Missing 'singlets' label.")
                continue

            # Filter valid
            valid_mask = (df[fsc_a] > 0) & (df[fsc_h] > 0)

            # Filter live cells if 'cells' column exists
            if 'cells' in df.columns:
                valid_mask = valid_mask & (df['cells'].astype(bool))

            clean_df = df.loc[valid_mask].copy()

            if len(clean_df) == 0:
                continue

            # Log Transform
            clean_df['Log_FSC_A'] = np.log10(clean_df[fsc_a])
            clean_df['Log_FSC_H'] = np.log10(clean_df[fsc_h])

            # Label
            # 'singlets' might be boolean or int. Convert to 1 (Singlet), 0 (Doublet)
            clean_df['Label'] = clean_df[label_col].astype(int)

            clean_df['DatasetID'] = dataset_counter
            clean_df['Filename'] = os.path.basename(file_path)
            clean_df['CellType'] = folder_name.lower()

            subset = clean_df[['Log_FSC_A', 'Log_FSC_H', 'Label', 'DatasetID', 'CellType']]

            # Optimize types
            subset['DatasetID'] = subset['DatasetID'].astype(np.int32)
            subset['Label'] = subset['Label'].astype(np.int8)
            subset['Log_FSC_A'] = subset['Log_FSC_A'].astype(np.float32)
            subset['Log_FSC_H'] = subset['Log_FSC_H'].astype(np.float32)

            dfs.append(subset)
            dataset_counter += 1

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error processing {file_path}: {e}")

    if not dfs:
        print("No valid data extracted.")
        return

    print("Concatenating...")
    full_df = pd.concat(dfs, ignore_index=True)

    print(f"Total samples: {len(full_df)}")
    print(f"Class Balance: {full_df['Label'].value_counts().to_dict()}")

    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(output_path), exist_ok=True)
    full_df.to_parquet(output_path, index=False)
    print(f"Saved to {output_path}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    generate_singlet_data()


==================================================
FILE: glow/active_learning/active_learning_scripts/train_full_model.py
==================================================
import sys
import os
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import joblib
from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split
from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score, classification_report
import shutil

# Ensure glow is in path
sys.path.append(os.getcwd())

from glow.active_learning.models import GatingClassifier

def train_and_plot():
    # Load data
    data_path = "data/real_training_data.parquet"
    print(f"Loading data from {data_path}...")

    if not os.path.exists(data_path):
        print("Error: Data file not found.")
        return

    # Load Full Dataset (Fits in memory check: 20M rows * ~40 bytes < 1GB)
    df = pd.read_parquet(data_path)
    print(f"Loaded {len(df)} samples.")
    print("Columns:", df.columns.tolist())

    # Pre-shuffling (Pandas sample with frac=1 shuffles rows)
    print("Shuffling data...")
    df = df.sample(frac=1, random_state=42).reset_index(drop=True)

    # Prepare Inputs
    # Features: Log_FSC, Log_SSC
    # Note: df already has Log_FSC, Log_SSC calculated.
    X = df[['Log_FSC', 'Log_SSC']].to_numpy()

    # Label: 0 or 1
    y = df['Label'].to_numpy()

    # Metadata for GatingClassifier
    dataset_ids = df['DatasetID'].to_numpy()
    cell_types = df['CellType'].to_numpy()

    # Split Train/Val (80/20)
    print("Splitting data 80/20...")
    X_train, X_val, y_train, y_val, id_train, id_val, ct_train, ct_val = train_test_split(
        X, y, dataset_ids, cell_types, test_size=0.2, random_state=42
    )

    print(f"Training set: {len(X_train)} samples")
    print(f"Validation set: {len(X_val)} samples")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Model Training
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    print("Initializing model...")
    # Using default architecture as defined in class
    model = GatingClassifier(random_state=42)
    model.mlp.verbose = True  # Let sklearn print progress
    model.mlp.early_stopping = True
    model.mlp.n_iter_no_change = 5

    print("Starting training (Standard fit)...")

    # model.fit expects: X, y, dataset_ids, cell_types
    model.fit(
        X_train,
        y_train,
        dataset_ids=id_train,
        cell_types=ct_train
    )

    print("Training complete.")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Evaluation
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    print("Evaluating on Validation Set...")
    y_pred = model.predict(X_val, dataset_ids=id_val, cell_types=ct_val)

    acc = accuracy_score(y_val, y_pred)
    print(f"Validation Accuracy: {acc:.4%}")
    print("\nClassification Report:")
    print(classification_report(y_val, y_pred, target_names=['Debris', 'Live']))

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Save Model
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    output_path = "data/gating_model_full.pkl"
    print(f"Saving model to {output_path}...")
    # Using joblib via pickle logic inside internal or external (joblib is standard for sklearn)
    # The user asked to save to data/
    with open(output_path, 'wb') as f:
        joblib.dump(model, f)

    print("Model saved.")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Plotting Loss Curve
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    if hasattr(model.mlp, 'loss_curve_'):
        print("Generating loss curve...")
        plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))
        plt.plot(model.mlp.loss_curve_, label='Training Loss')

        # If early stopping was used, validation scores might be available in validation_scores_
        if hasattr(model.mlp, 'validation_scores_') and model.mlp.validation_scores_:
             plt.plot(model.mlp.validation_scores_, label='Validation Score (Accuracy)')
             plt.legend()

        plt.title('MLP Training Loss Curve')
        plt.xlabel('Iterations')
        plt.ylabel('Loss')
        plt.grid(True)

        plot_path = "data/training_loss_curve.png"
        plt.savefig(plot_path)
        print(f"Saved loss curve to {plot_path}")
    else:
        print("Warning: Loss curve not found in model attributes.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    train_and_plot()


==================================================
FILE: debug/verify_comp_math.py
==================================================
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np

def verify_math():
    print("Verifying Compensation Math...")
    
    # 1. Simulate Source Spills
    # FITC spills 20% into PE.
    # PE spills 0% into FITC.
    
    # True Signals
    # Event 1: Pure FITC 1000.
    # Event 2: Pure PE 1000.
    true_data = pd.DataFrame({
        'FITC': [1000., 0.],
        'PE':   [0., 1000.]
    })
    
    # Spillover Matrix S
    # Row=Source, Col=Detector
    # S_fitc_fitc = 1, S_fitc_pe = 0.2
    # S_pe_fitc = 0, S_pe_pe = 1
    
    S = pd.DataFrame([
        [1.0, 0.2], # FITC Source
        [0.0, 1.0]  # PE Source (Note: If PE spilled into FITC, this would be [0.05, 1.0])
    ], index=['FITC', 'PE'], columns=['FITC', 'PE'])
    
    print("Spillover Matrix S:")
    print(S)
    
    # Observe Data = True * S (Pandas: True.dot(S))
    # True (N x F). S (F x D).
    observed_data = true_data.dot(S)
    print("\nObserved Data (with spillover):")
    print(observed_data)
    # Event 1: FITC=1000, PE=200. (Correct 20% spill)
    # Event 2: FITC=0, PE=1000.
    
    # 2. Compensation
    # C = Inv(S)
    C_values = np.linalg.inv(S.values)
    C = pd.DataFrame(C_values, index=['FITC', 'PE'], columns=['FITC', 'PE'])
    
    print("\nCompensation Matrix C = Inv(S):")
    print(C)
    # Inverse of [[1, 0.2], [0, 1]] is [[1, -0.2], [0, 1]]
    
    # 3. Apply Compensation
    # Corrected = Observed * C
    # This seems to be the contentious part.
    # Is it Observed * C? Or Observed * C.T?
    
    # Let's try Observed.dot(C)
    compensated_1 = observed_data.dot(C)
    print("\nAttempt 1: Observed.dot(C)")
    print(compensated_1)
    
    # Check Result
    # Ev 1 (FITC only): Should be [1000, 0].
    # Obs: [1000, 200].
    # C: [[1, -0.2], [0, 1]] (Rows=Source? Cols=Det?)
    # Wait.
    # If C = Inv(S). C has same index/column structure as S.
    # S: Index=Fluor, Col=Det.
    # C: Index=Fluor, Col=Det.
    
    # Dot Product: A.dot(B) -> Sum over A's axis 1 (Cols) and B's axis 0 (Index).
    # A (Observed) Cols = Detectors (FITC, PE).
    # B (C) Index = Fluorophores? (FITC, PE).
    # Since names match, it aligns.
    
    # Calculation for Ev 1 [1000, 200] dot C:
    # Res_FITC (Col 0 of C): 1000*C[0,0] + 200*C[1,0] = 1000*1 + 200*0 = 1000.
    # Res_PE   (Col 1 of C): 1000*C[0,1] + 200*C[1,1] = 1000*(-0.2) + 200*1 = -200 + 200 = 0.
    
    # This works Perfect.
    
    # 4. What if values were swapped?
    # S = [[1, 0], [0.2, 1]] (PE spills to FITC)
    # C = [[1, 0], [-0.2, 1]]
    
    # Observed PE event (0, 1000) -> Spills to FITC 200. Obs: [200, 1000].
    # Comp: [200, 1000] dot [[1, 0], [-0.2, 1]]
    # Res_FITC: 200*1 + 1000*(-0.2) = 200 - 200 = 0.
    # Res_PE:   200*0 + 1000*1 = 1000.
    # Works.
    
    print("Math verification passed.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    verify_math()


==================================================
FILE: debug/visualize_overlap.py
==================================================
import sys
import os
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import joblib

# Ensure glow is in path
sys.path.append(os.getcwd())

from glow.active_learning.models import GatingClassifier, DensityCenteringTransformer

def visualize_dataset_overlap():
    data_path = "data/real_training_data.parquet"
    if not os.path.exists(data_path):
        print("Data not found")
        return

    # Load small subset
    df = pd.read_parquet(data_path).sample(50000, random_state=42)

    # Pick one dataset ID that has decent count
    counts = df['DatasetID'].value_counts()
    target_id = counts.index[0]

    subset = df[df['DatasetID'] == target_id].copy()
    print(f"Visualizing DatasetID {target_id} with {len(subset)} samples.")

    X = subset[['Log_FSC', 'Log_SSC']].to_numpy()
    y = subset['Label'].to_numpy()

    # Apply Centering
    centerer = DensityCenteringTransformer()
    X_centered = centerer.transform(X) # No ID needed since single dataset

    # Calculate Separation
    live = X_centered[y == 1]
    debris = X_centered[y == 0]

    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 5))

    plt.subplot(1, 2, 1)
    plt.scatter(debris[:, 0], debris[:, 1], c='red', s=1, alpha=0.5, label='Debris')
    plt.scatter(live[:, 0], live[:, 1], c='blue', s=1, alpha=0.5, label='Live')
    plt.title("Original (Centered) Features")
    plt.xlabel("Centered Log FSC")
    plt.ylabel("Centered Log SSC")
    plt.legend()

    # Histogram of Distance from Center
    plt.subplot(1, 2, 2)
    dist_live = np.linalg.norm(live, axis=1)
    dist_debris = np.linalg.norm(debris, axis=1)

    plt.hist(dist_live, bins=50, color='blue', alpha=0.5, density=True, label='Live')
    plt.hist(dist_debris, bins=50, color='red', alpha=0.5, density=True, label='Debris')
    plt.title("Distance from Mode (0,0)")
    plt.xlabel("Euclidean Distance")
    plt.legend()

    plt.savefig("debug/overlap_check.png")
    print("Saved plot to debug/overlap_check.png")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    visualize_dataset_overlap()

import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
from glow.analysis.positive import calculate_positive_stats

def test_positive_stats():
    # Load the test data
    csv_path = "./tests/test_data/experiment_full_population.csv.gz"
    print(f"Loading {csv_path}...")
    df = pd.read_csv(csv_path)

    # Mock Experiment
    class MockExperiment:
        def __init__(self, data):
            self.data = data
            self.conditions = {'Sample': 'Sample'} # Adjust if Sample col exists
            # Create a simple mapping if Sample column exists, else 1 sample
            if 'Sample' not in self.data.columns and 'sample' not in self.data.columns:
                 self.data['Sample'] = 'TestSample'

    experiment = MockExperiment(df)

    # Pick a channel - finding a numeric one
    exclude = ['cells', 'singlets', 'Sample', 'sample', 'Cell_Line', 'Replicate', 'Experiment_Name', 'Event Info']
    channels = [c for c in df.columns if c not in exclude and pd.api.types.is_numeric_dtype(df[c])]

    if not channels:
        print("No numeric channels found.")
        return

    target_channel = channels[0]
    print(f"Testing on channel: {target_channel}")

    # ----------------------------------------------------
    # DIAGNOSTIC: Check Live/Singlet logic manually
    # ----------------------------------------------------
    total_events = len(df)

    # Check 'cells' column
    if 'cells' in df.columns:
        n_cells = df['cells'].sum()
        print(f"Column 'cells': {n_cells}/{total_events} ({(n_cells/total_events)*100:.2f}%)")
    else:
        print("Column 'cells' not found.")

    # Check 'singlets' column
    if 'singlets' in df.columns:
        n_singlets = df['singlets'].sum()
        print(f"Column 'singlets': {n_singlets}/{total_events} ({(n_singlets/total_events)*100:.2f}%)")
    else:
        print("Column 'singlets' not found.")

    # Check Intersection
    if 'cells' in df.columns and 'singlets' in df.columns:
        n_valid = (df['cells'] & df['singlets']).sum()
        print(f"Intersection (cells & singlets): {n_valid}/{total_events} ({(n_valid/total_events)*100:.2f}%)")
        print(f"Singlets % (of Live): {(n_valid/n_cells)*100:.2f}%")

    # ----------------------------------------------------
    # Run Tool Logic
    # ----------------------------------------------------

    # Data stats
    vals = df[target_channel].values

    min_v, max_v = vals.min(), vals.max()
    mean_v = vals.mean()

    print(f"Range: {min_v} - {max_v}, Mean: {mean_v}")

    # Test Thresholds
    # 1. Min - Should be near 100%
    t_min = min_v - 1
    res_min = calculate_positive_stats(experiment, target_channel, t_min)
    print(f"Threshold {t_min} (All > T): \n{res_min}")

    # 2. Max - Should be 0%
    t_max = max_v + 1
    res_max = calculate_positive_stats(experiment, target_channel, t_max)
    print(f"Threshold {t_max} (All > T): \n{res_max}")

    # 3. Mean - Should be mixed
    t_mean = mean_v
    res_mean = calculate_positive_stats(experiment, target_channel, t_mean)
    print(f"Threshold {t_mean} (All > T): \n{res_mean}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_positive_stats()

import cytoflow
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
import logging
import sys
import os

# Add project root to path
sys.path.append(os.getcwd())

from glow.compensation.manager import CompensationManager

# Setup Logging
logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

from glow.data_loading.loader import DataLoader

def run_test():
    print("Loading Files with Glow Loader...")
    
    loader = DataLoader()
    
    # Prepare preview metadata manually
    # tests/test_complex_upload/no_stain.fcs -> Unstained
    # tests/test_complex_upload/GFP_only_C10.fcs -> GFP_Control
    # tests/test_complex_upload/BFP_only.fcs -> BFP_Control
    
    meta_list = [
        {'original_path': 'tests/test_complex_upload/no_stain.fcs', 'display_name': 'Unstained', 'current_path': 'tests/test_complex_upload/no_stain.fcs', 'condition': 'Control'},
        {'original_path': 'tests/test_complex_upload/GFP_only_C10.fcs', 'display_name': 'GFP_Control', 'current_path': 'tests/test_complex_upload/GFP_only_C10.fcs', 'condition': 'Control'},
        {'original_path': 'tests/test_complex_upload/BFP_only.fcs', 'display_name': 'BFP_Control', 'current_path': 'tests/test_complex_upload/BFP_only.fcs', 'condition': 'Control'}
    ]
    
    experiment = loader.load_as_cytoflow_experiment(meta_list)
    
    if experiment is None:
        print("Loader failed.")
        return

    print(f"Loaded {len(experiment.data)} events.")
    
    # 2. Setup Manager
    manager = CompensationManager()
    channels = ['GFP-A', 'TagBFP-A'] 
    
    unstained_spec = {'Sample': 'Unstained'}
    controls_spec = {
        'GFP-A': {'Sample': 'GFP_Control'},
        'TagBFP-A': {'Sample': 'BFP_Control'}
    }
    
    # 3. Compute Matrix
    print("Computing Matrix...")
    matrix = manager.compute_spillover_matrix(experiment, unstained_spec, controls_spec, channels)
    print("\nSpillover Matrix:")
    print(matrix)
    
    # 4. Apply Compensation
    print("Applying Compensation...")
    comp_df = manager.apply_compensation(experiment.data, channels, rename=True)
    
    # 5. Check Results (GFP -> BFP Spillover)
    # We look at the GFP Control. 
    # Ideally: comp_TagBFP-A should be flat (same median as Unstained).
    
    # Get Unstained Median BFP
    mask_u = comp_df['Sample'] == 'Unstained'
    u_bfp = comp_df.loc[mask_u, 'comp_TagBFP-A'].median()
    
    # Get GFP Control Median BFP (Compensated)
    # Filter for high GFP events 
    mask_g_pos = (comp_df['Sample'] == 'GFP_Control') & (comp_df['GFP-A'] > 1e5)
    if mask_g_pos.sum() == 0:
        print("Warning: No GFP+ events found > 1e5")
        g_bfp = 0
    else:
        g_bfp = comp_df.loc[mask_g_pos, 'comp_TagBFP-A'].median()
        
    # Also check the "Negative" population of the GFP Control
    # To see if the baseline was shifted?
    mask_g_neg = (comp_df['Sample'] == 'GFP_Control') & (comp_df['GFP-A'] < 1e4) # Low GFP
    if mask_g_neg.sum() > 0:
        g_neg_bfp = comp_df.loc[mask_g_neg, 'comp_TagBFP-A'].median()
    else:
        g_neg_bfp = 0
    
    print(f"\n[Compensated Stats]")
    print(f"Unstained BFP Median:       {u_bfp:.2f}")
    print(f"GFP-Control (Pos) BFP Median: {g_bfp:.2f}")
    print(f"GFP-Control (Neg) BFP Median: {g_neg_bfp:.2f}")
    
    diff = g_bfp - u_bfp
    print(f"Difference (Pos - Unstained): {diff:.2f}")

    # Inspect the internal logic values by checking logs or hacking
    # We can check specific values here
    u_raw = experiment.data.loc[experiment.data['Sample']=='Unstained', channels].median()
    g_raw = experiment.data.loc[experiment.data['Sample']=='GFP_Control']
    
    print("\n[Raw Stats]")
    print("Unstained Medians:\n", u_raw)
    
    q_lo = g_raw['GFP-A'].quantile(0.2)
    g_neg = g_raw[g_raw['GFP-A'] <= q_lo][channels].median()
    print("GFP_Control Low (Internal Neg):\n", g_neg)

    # Internal Negative logic check
    internal_neg_bfp = g_neg['TagBFP-A']
    diff_internal = abs(g_bfp - internal_neg_bfp)
    print(f"Difference (Pos - Internal Neg): {diff_internal:.2f}")

    if abs(diff) < 2000:
        print("SUCCESS: Compensated to Unstained Baseline.")
    elif diff_internal < 25000: 
        print(f"SUCCESS: Compensated close to Sample-Specific Background ({internal_neg_bfp:.0f}).")
        print("Note: The shift from Unstained (15k) to Sample Neg (50k) is preserved.")
    elif g_bfp < u_bfp - 2000:
        print("FAIL: OVERCOMPENSATION (Dip > 2000 below Unstained)")
    else:
        print(f"FAIL: UNDERCOMPENSATION (Pos {g_bfp:.0f} != Neg {internal_neg_bfp:.0f})")
    
    # Check Max values
    max_gfp = experiment.data['GFP-A'].max()
    print(f"Max GFP: {max_gfp}")
    
    # Check Pos Stats used for Slope
    # Recalculate manually to see what Manager saw
    # Mocking logic
    gfp_tube = experiment.data[experiment.data['Sample']=='GFP_Control']
    q_hi = gfp_tube['GFP-A'].quantile(0.95)
    print(f"Q95 GFP: {q_hi}")
    
    top_5 = gfp_tube[gfp_tube['GFP-A'] >= q_hi]
    print(f"Top 5% GFP Median: {top_5['GFP-A'].median()}")
    print(f"Top 5% BFP Median: {top_5['TagBFP-A'].median()}")
    
    internal_low = gfp_tube[gfp_tube['GFP-A'] <= gfp_tube['GFP-A'].quantile(0.2)]
    print(f"Low 20% BFP Median: {internal_low['TagBFP-A'].median()}")
    
    # Calculate Slope manually
    num = top_5['TagBFP-A'].median() - internal_low['TagBFP-A'].median()
    denom = top_5['GFP-A'].median() - internal_low['GFP-A'].median()
    slope = num / denom
    print(f"Calculated Slope: {slope}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_test()

import cytoflow
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
from glow.gating.manager import GatingManager

def test_gating():
    print("Loading data...")
    tube = cytoflow.Tube(file='data/01-Well-A1.fcs')
    op = cytoflow.ImportOp(tubes=[tube])
    experiment = op.apply()
    
    print("Channels:", experiment.channels)
    
    # Verify data stats
    print(experiment.data[['FSC-A', 'SSC-A']].describe())
    
    gater = GatingManager()
    
    print("Attempting Auto Gating (Density)...")
    try:
        # Simulate empty experiment
        empty_data = experiment.data.iloc[:0]
        empty_exp = experiment.clone()
        empty_exp.data = empty_data
        
        gated_exp = gater.auto_gate_live_cells(
            empty_exp, 
            x_channel="FSC-A", 
            y_channel="SSC-A", 
            method="density",
            keep=0.9,
            sigma=2.0
        )
        print("Density Gating (Empty) Success")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Density Gating (Empty) Failed: {e}")

    print("Attempting Auto Gating (All NaN)...")
    try:
        # Simulate All NaN
        nan_data = experiment.data.copy()
        nan_data['FSC-A'] = np.nan
        nan_exp = experiment.clone()
        nan_exp.data = nan_data
        
        gated_exp = gater.auto_gate_live_cells(
            nan_exp, 
            x_channel="FSC-A", 
            y_channel="SSC-A", 
            method="density",
            keep=0.9,
            sigma=2.0
        )
        print("Density Gating (NaN) Success")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Density Gating (NaN) Failed: {e}")


    print("Attempting Auto Gating (GMM)...")
    try:
        gated_exp = gater.auto_gate_live_cells(
            experiment, 
            x_channel="FSC-A", 
            y_channel="SSC-A", 
            method="gmm",
            num_components=2
        )
        print("GMM Gating Success")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"GMM Gating Failed: {e}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_gating()

import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import os

def compare_distributions():
    real_path = "data/real_data.parquet"
    synth_path = "data/synthetic_training_data.parquet"

    print(f"Loading {real_path}...")
    try:
        df_real = pd.read_parquet(real_path)
        print(f"Loaded Real Data: {df_real.shape}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error loading real data: {e}")
        return

    print(f"Loading {synth_path}...")
    try:
        df_synth = pd.read_parquet(synth_path)
        print(f"Loaded Synth Data: {df_synth.shape}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error loading synth data: {e}")
        return

    features = ["FSC-A", "SSC-A"]

    # Statistics
    print("\n--- Statistics (Linear Scale) ---")
    for feat in features:
        print(f"\nFeature: {feat}")
        print("Real Data:")
        print(df_real[feat].describe())
        print("Synthetic Data:")
        print(df_synth[feat].describe())

    # Log Statistics
    print("\n--- Statistics (Log1p Scale) ---")
    for feat in features:
        feat_log = f"Log_{feat}"
        # Calculate log if not present
        real_log = np.log1p(df_real[feat])
        synth_log = np.log1p(df_synth[feat])

        print(f"\nFeature: {feat_log}")
        print("Real Data:")
        print(real_log.describe())
        print("Synthetic Data:")
        print(synth_log.describe())

    # Plotting
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(15, 10))

    for i, feat in enumerate(features):
        # Linear Plot
        ax_lin = axes[0, i]
        ax_lin.hist(df_synth[feat], bins=50, alpha=0.5, label='Synthetic', density=True, color='blue')
        ax_lin.hist(df_real[feat], bins=50, alpha=0.5, label='Real', density=True, color='red')
        ax_lin.set_title(f"{feat} (Linear)")
        ax_lin.legend()

        # Log Plot
        ax_log = axes[1, i]
        real_log = np.log1p(df_real[feat])
        synth_log = np.log1p(df_synth[feat])

        ax_log.hist(synth_log, bins=50, alpha=0.5, label='Synthetic', density=True, color='blue')
        ax_log.hist(real_log, bins=50, alpha=0.5, label='Real', density=True, color='red')
        ax_log.set_title(f"{feat} (Log1p)")
        ax_log.legend()

    output_file = "debug/distribution_comparison.png"
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(output_file)
    print(f"\nPlot saved to {output_file}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    compare_distributions()

import sys
import os
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np

# Mock Streamlit to avoid errors during import
import unittest.mock as mock
mock_st = mock.MagicMock()
sys.modules["streamlit"] = mock_st

# Setup paths
sys.path.append(os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.getcwd(), '..')))
sys.path.append(os.getcwd())

from glow.plotting.scatter import plot_2d_scatter_plotly
import plotly.graph_objects as go
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

# Create Dummy Experiment
class Experiment:
    def __init__(self):
        self.data = pd.DataFrame({
            "FSC-A": np.random.rand(100) * 1000,
            "SSC-A": np.random.rand(100) * 1000,
            "CD3": np.random.rand(100) * 100,
            "Sample": ["S1"] * 50 + ["S2"] * 50
        })

exp = Experiment()

def test_plotly_generation():
    print("Testing Plotly Generation...")
    fig = plot_2d_scatter_plotly(exp, "FSC-A", "SSC-A", hue="Sample", title="Test Plot")
    assert isinstance(fig, go.Figure)
    print("Plotly Figure Created Successfully.")
    
    # Check layout
    assert fig.layout.title.text == "Test Plot"
    print("Title correct.")

def test_matplotlib_scaling_logic():
    print("Testing Matplotlib Scaling Logic...")
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(6, 4))
    ax.scatter([1, 2], [3, 4])
    
    # Simulate Canvas Logic
    font_scale = 2.0
    width_scale = 1.5
    height_scale = 0.5
    
    w_orig, h_orig = fig.get_size_inches()
    
    target_w = (w_orig * width_scale) / font_scale
    target_h = (h_orig * height_scale) / font_scale
    
    fig.set_size_inches(target_w, target_h)
    
    current_w, current_h = fig.get_size_inches()
    print(f"Original: {w_orig}x{h_orig}, Target: {target_w}x{target_h}, Result: {current_w}x{current_h}")
    
    assert np.isclose(current_w, target_w)
    assert np.isclose(current_h, target_h)
    print("Matplotlib Scaling Logic Verified.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        test_plotly_generation()
        test_matplotlib_scaling_logic()
        print("\nAll Tests Passed!")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"\nTest Failed: {e}")
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
